```
     1→export function buildExtractionSystemPrompt(input: { onboarding: boolean }): string {
     2→  return [
     3→    "Extract structured business entities and relationships from the provided text.",
     4→    "Return only high-confidence extractions with explicit entity references.",
     5→    "Entity kinds: project, feature, task, decision, question.",
     6→    "Never emit kind: person.",
     7→    "If a person is explicitly tied to an entity, set assignee_name on that entity using the literal source name.",
     8→    "",
     9→    "Output schema rules:",
    10→    "- Each entity must include tempId, kind, text, confidence, evidence. For task, decision, and question entities also include category.",
    11→    "- Entity text must be specific and concrete, not generic placeholders.",
    12→    "- evidence must be a verbatim snippet from Current source text.",
    13→    "- Always include tools and relationships arrays (can be empty).",
    14→    "- confidence is a number between 0 and 1.",
    15→    "",
    16→    "Source grounding and context rules:",
    17→    "- When Current message metadata exists, extract from Current source text only.",
    18→    "- Conversation history is only for reference resolution (that/it/this/first option).",
    19→    "- History must never add new entities that are absent from Current source text intent.",
    20→    "- Do not copy assistant-only rationale/details from history into entity text.",
    21→    "- Use the shortest canonical concept phrase when resolving references.",
    22→    "- If entity meaning requires context resolution, include resolvedFromMessageId as the raw message id string only (UUID).",
    23→    "- Never include wrappers or prefixes in resolvedFromMessageId (no [message:...], no message:...).",
    24→    "- resolvedFromMessageId must be one of the allowed history message ids listed in the prompt section; otherwise omit it.",
    25→    "- Prefer user messages as resolvedFromMessageId anchors when both user and assistant mention the same concept.",
    26→    "- Never set resolvedFromMessageId to Current message id.",
    27→    "- If Current message metadata is unavailable (document chunk extraction), do not emit resolvedFromMessageId.",
    28→    "- If a confirmation cannot resolve to one specific concept, emit no entity.",
    29→    "",
    30→    "Kind classification rules:",
    31→    "- Project: named product, system, or initiative that is a top-level workstream within the workspace (building, launching, creating, developing; identified by a proper noun or product name).",
    32→    "- Decision: commitment/selection language (let's go with, move forward with, we decided, we'll use, I'm choosing, going with, settled on, committed to, approved).",
    33→    "- Task: concrete executable work with an action verb (implement, build, fix, migrate, deploy, set up, write, test, review, handle, research, investigate, evaluate, analyze, explore).",
    34→    "- Feature: capability/requirement or scoped artifact description (supports, provides, enables, needs to handle, must support; cards/dashboards/flows/screens/integrations).",
    35→    "- Question: explicit question framing or question mark.",
    36→    "- Option selection between alternatives is decision, not task.",
    37→    "- Prioritization/scoping statements about a specific capability without explicit execution action are feature (not project-level initiatives).",
    38→    "",
    39→    "Category classification rules:",
    40→    "- For task, decision, and question entities, classify category based on the area the entity affects.",
    41→    "- Categories: engineering, research, marketing, operations, design, sales.",
    42→    "- engineering: building, implementing, fixing, deploying code.",
    43→    "- research: investigating, evaluating, comparing, exploring unknowns.",
    44→    "- marketing: outreach, content, positioning, launch activities.",
    45→    "- operations: setup, configuration, process, infrastructure admin.",
    46→    "- design: wireframes, prototypes, UX, visual design, user flows.",
    47→    "- sales: prospect outreach, demos, pitching, qualifying leads, choosing target segments, defining ICP, go-to-market targeting.",
    48→    "- Classify by what the entity affects, not where it came up. 'Use JWT' is engineering even if discussed during design.",
    49→    "- Set category to 'none' for project and feature entities.",
    50→    "",
    51→    "Project vs feature scope rule (overrides kind classification artifact hints):",
    52→    "- Use the Workspace scope section to determine hierarchy level.",
    53→    "- Entities at the top level of a workspace are projects. Capabilities within an existing project are features.",
    54→    "- If the workspace itself is the product, its major workstreams are projects.",
    55→    "- When workspace scope lists no existing projects, a new independent capability is a project regardless of artifact-type nouns (dashboard, flow, module, etc.). This rule only applies when workspace scope is present.",
    56→    "- If projects already exist, new capabilities described within them are features.",
    57→    "- A clearly independent domain or workstream (e.g. compliance reporting, analytics) alongside existing projects is a new project, not a feature of those projects.",
    58→    "- A project would have features underneath it; a feature would have tasks underneath it.",
    59→    "",
    60→    "Question option suppression:",
    61→    "- If one question includes options (X or Y), extract one question only.",
    62→    "- Do not extract the options as separate decision/task entities.",
    63→    "",

    91→    "- Input: My project needs work. => entities: none; tools: none.",
    92→    "- Input: The feature is broken. => entities: none; tools: none.",
```

```
     1→import { createScorer } from "evalite";
     2→import type { GoldenCase, ExtractionEvalOutput } from "../types";
     3→import { matchesExpectedEntity } from "./shared";
     4→
     5→export const categoryAccuracyScorer = createScorer<GoldenCase, ExtractionEvalOutput, GoldenCase>({
     6→  name: "category-accuracy",
     7→  description: "Percent of expected entities with expectedCategory whose matched extraction has the correct category.",
     8→  scorer: ({ output, expected }) => {
     9→    const expectedEntities = expected?.expectedEntities ?? [];
    10→    const withCategory = expectedEntities.filter((e) => e.expectedCategory);
    11→    if (withCategory.length === 0) {
    12→      return { score: 1 };
    13→    }
    14→
    15→    let matched = 0;
    16→    for (const expectedEntity of withCategory) {
    17→      const found = output.extractedEntities.find((entity) =>
    18→        matchesExpectedEntity(expectedEntity, entity, 0.5),
    19→      );
    20→      if (found && found.category === expectedEntity.expectedCategory) {
    21→        matched += 1;
    22→      }
    23→    }
    24→
    25→    return { score: matched / withCategory.length };
    26→  },
    27→});
    28→
```

```
    22→import { extractStructuredGraph } from "../app/src/server/extraction/extract-graph";
    23→import { persistExtractionOutput, appendExtractedTools } from "../app/src/server/extraction/persist-extraction";
    24→import { loadExtractionConversationContext, loadConversationGraphContext } from "../app/src/server/extraction/context-loaders";
    25→import type { SourceRecord } from "../app/src/server/extraction/types";
    26→import { loadWorkspaceProjects } from "../app/src/server/workspace/workspace-scope";
    27→import {
    28→  type EvalRuntime,
    29→  setupEvalRuntime,
    30→  teardownEvalRuntime,
    31→  seedWorkspace,
    32→  seedConversationContext,
    33→  seedUserMessage,
    34→  seedGraphEntities,
    35→  loadWorkspacePeopleCount,
    36→  createDeterministicIdGenerator,
    37→} from "./eval-test-kit";
    38→
    39→const extractionModel = process.env.EXTRACTION_MODEL ?? "anthropic/claude-3.5-haiku";
    40→const autoevalModel = requireEnv("AUTOEVAL_MODEL");
    41→
    42→const cacheDir = process.env.EVAL_CACHE_DIR ?? "eval-results/cache";
    43→const cachePath = join(cacheDir, "extraction-cache.json");
    44→const resultCache = loadCache(cachePath);
    45→const cases = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(process.cwd(), "evals", "data", "golden-cases.json"), "utf8")) as GoldenCase[];
    46→assertAutoevalEnv();
    47→
    48→// strict_single is reserved for unambiguous one-entity probes.
    49→// Cases where multiple extractions are semantically valid should use multi_allowed.
    50→const intentScoreWeights: Record<
    51→  GoldenCaseIntent,
    52→  Record<
    53→    | "entity-precision"
    54→    | "entity-recall"
    55→    | "no-extra-entities"
    56→    | "no-phantom-persons"
    57→    | "evidence-grounded"
    58→    | "no-context-bleed"
    59→    | "evidence-source-current-message"
    60→    | "resolved-from-lineage"
    61→    | "tool-filtering"
    62→    | "forbidden-kinds"
    63→    | "factuality"
    64→    | "relation-recall"
    65→    | "category-accuracy",
    66→    number
    67→  >
    68→> = {
    69→  strict_single: {
    70→    "entity-precision": 0.16,
    71→    "entity-recall": 0.16,
    72→    "no-extra-entities": 0.18,
    73→    "no-phantom-persons": 0.12,
    74→    "evidence-grounded": 0.08,
    75→    "no-context-bleed": 0.05,
    76→    "evidence-source-current-message": 0.05,
    77→    "resolved-from-lineage": 0.04,
    78→    "tool-filtering": 0.06,
    79→    "forbidden-kinds": 0.08,
    80→    factuality: 0.02,
    81→    "relation-recall": 0,
    82→    "category-accuracy": 0.1,
    83→  },
    84→  multi_allowed: {
    85→    "entity-precision": 0.21,
    86→    "entity-recall": 0.21,
    87→    "no-extra-entities": 0.05,
    88→    "no-phantom-persons": 0.11,
    89→    "evidence-grounded": 0.08,
    90→    "no-context-bleed": 0.04,
    91→    "evidence-source-current-message": 0.06,
    92→    "resolved-from-lineage": 0.04,
    93→    "tool-filtering": 0.04,
    94→    "forbidden-kinds": 0.06,
    95→    factuality: 0.02,
    96→    "relation-recall": 0.03,
    97→    "category-accuracy": 0.1,
    98→  },
    99→};
   100→
   101→let runtime: EvalRuntime;
   102→
   103→beforeAll(async () => {
   104→  runtime = await setupEvalRuntime("extraction");
   105→}, 120_000);
   106→
   107→afterAll(async () => {
   108→  await teardownEvalRuntime(runtime);
   109→}, 120_000);
   110→
   111→const factualityScorer = createScorer<GoldenCase, ExtractionEvalOutput, GoldenCase>({
   112→  name: "factuality",
   113→  description: "Average factual grounding score for extracted entity texts against matched provenance evidence snippets.",
   114→  scorer: async ({ input, output }) => {
   115→    if (output.extractedEntities.length === 0) {
   116→      return { score: 1 };
   117→    }
   118→
   119→    let total = 0;
   120→    for (const entity of output.extractedEntities) {
   121→      const snippet = resolveEntityEvidenceSnippet(entity.text, output.evidenceRows, input.input);
   122→      const result = await Factuality({
   123→        input: snippet,
   124→        output: entity.text,
   125→        expected: snippet,
   126→        model: autoevalModel,
   127→      });
   128→      total += result.score ?? 0;
   129→    }
   130→
   131→    return { score: total / output.extractedEntities.length };
   132→  },
   133→});
   134→
   135→evalite<GoldenCase, ExtractionEvalOutput, GoldenCase>("Extraction Golden Cases", {
   136→  data: cases.map((testCase) => ({ input: testCase, expected: testCase })),
   137→  task: async (input) => runCase(input),
   138→  scorers: [
   139→    entityPrecisionScorer,
   140→    entityRecallScorer,
   141→    noExtraEntitiesScorer,
   142→    noPhantomPersonsScorer,
   143→    evidenceGroundedScorer,
   144→    noContextBleedScorer,
   145→    evidenceSourceCurrentMessageScorer,
   146→    resolvedFromLineageScorer,
   147→    toolFilteringScorer,
   148→    forbiddenKindsScorer,
   149→    relationRecallScorer,
   150→    categoryAccuracyScorer,
   151→    factualityScorer,
   152→  ],
   153→  columns: ({ input, output, scores }) => [
   154→    { label: "Case", value: input.id },
   155→    { label: "Intent", value: input.intent },
   156→    { label: "Ent", value: `${output.extractedEntities.length}/${input.expectedEntities.length}` },
   157→    { label: "Prec", value: formatScoreCell(scoreByName(scores, "entity-precision")) },
   158→    { label: "Rec", value: formatScoreCell(scoreByName(scores, "entity-recall")) },
   159→    { label: "Cat", value: formatScoreCell(scoreByName(scores, "category-accuracy")) },
   160→    {
   161→      label: "Avg",
   162→      value: computeWeightedAverage(input.intent, scores).toFixed(2),
   163→    },
   164→  ],
   165→});
   166→
   167→function scoreByName(
   168→  scores: Array<{ name: string; score: number | null }>,
   169→  name: string,
   170→): number {
   171→  const match = scores.find((score) => score.name === name);
   172→  if (!match || match.score === null || Number.isNaN(match.score)) {
   173→    throw new Error(`Missing score for scorer: ${name}`);
   174→  }
   175→
   176→  return match.score;
   177→}
   178→
   179→function formatScoreCell(value: number): string {
   180→  return value.toFixed(2);
   181→}
   182→
   183→function computeWeightedAverage(
   184→  intent: GoldenCaseIntent,
   185→  scores: Array<{ name: string; score: number | null }>,
   186→): number {
   187→  const weights = intentScoreWeights[intent];
   188→  if (!weights) {
   189→    throw new Error(`Unknown golden case intent: ${intent}`);
   190→  }
   191→
   192→  let weightedTotal = 0;
   193→  let weightTotal = 0;
   194→  for (const [scorerName, weight] of Object.entries(weights)) {
   195→    if (weight <= 0) {
   196→      continue;
   197→    }
   198→
   199→    weightedTotal += scoreByName(scores, scorerName) * weight;
   200→    weightTotal += weight;
   201→  }
   202→
   203→  if (weightTotal === 0) {
   204→    throw new Error(`No scorer weights configured for intent: ${intent}`);
   205→  }
   206→
   207→  return weightedTotal / weightTotal;
   208→}
   209→
   210→function resolveEntityEvidenceSnippet(
   211→  entityText: string,
   212→  evidenceRows: Array<{ evidence?: string; fromText?: string; model?: string }>,
   213→  fallbackInput: string,
   214→): string {
   215→  const normalizedEntityText = normalizeForSubstring(entityText);
   216→  const matched = evidenceRows.find((row) => normalizeForSubstring(row.fromText ?? "") === normalizedEntityText);
   217→  const snippet = matched?.evidence ?? evidenceRows[0]?.evidence ?? fallbackInput;
   218→  return snippet.trim().length > 0 ? snippet : fallbackInput;
   219→}
   220→
   221→async function runCase(testCase: GoldenCase): Promise<ExtractionEvalOutput> {
   222→  const cacheKey = buildCaseCacheKey(extractionModel, testCase);
   223→  const cached = resultCache[cacheKey];
   224→  if (cached) {
   225→    return {
   226→      ...cached,
   227→      extractedTools: cached.extractedTools ?? [],
   228→      extractedRelations: cached.extractedRelations ?? [],
   229→    };
   230→  }
   231→
   232→  const nextId = createDeterministicIdGenerator(testCase.id);
   233→  const { workspaceRecord, workspaceName, projectRecord, conversationRecord, ownerPersonCount } = await seedWorkspace(runtime.surreal, testCase.workspace_name, nextId);
   234→  const conversationId = conversationRecord.id as string;
   235→  const seededContext = testCase.context ?? [];
   236→  const contextMessageIds = seededContext.length > 0
   237→    ? await seedConversationContext(runtime.surreal, conversationRecord, seededContext, nextId)
   238→    : [];
   239→
   240→  if (testCase.workspace_seed && testCase.workspace_seed.length > 0) {
   241→    await seedGraphEntities(runtime.surreal, workspaceRecord, projectRecord, conversationRecord, testCase.workspace_seed, nextId);
   242→  }
   243→
   244→  const userMessageRecord = await seedUserMessage(runtime.surreal, conversationRecord, testCase.input, nextId);
   245→
   246→  const extractionConversationContext = await loadExtractionConversationContext({
   247→    surreal: runtime.surreal,
   248→    conversationId,
   249→    currentMessageRecord: userMessageRecord,
   250→  });
   251→  const extractionGraphContext = await loadConversationGraphContext(runtime.surreal, conversationId, 60);
   252→  const workspaceProjects = await loadWorkspaceProjects(runtime.surreal, workspaceRecord);
   253→  const workspaceProjectNames = workspaceProjects.map((project) => project.name);
   254→
   255→  const extraction = await extractStructuredGraph({
   256→    extractionModel: runtime.extractionModel,
   257→    conversationHistory: extractionConversationContext.conversationHistory,
   258→    currentMessage: extractionConversationContext.currentMessage,
   259→    graphContext: extractionGraphContext,
   260→    sourceText: testCase.input,
   261→    onboarding: true,
   262→    workspaceName,
   263→    projectNames: workspaceProjectNames,
   264→  });
   265→
   266→  const now = new Date();
   267→  const persistence = await persistExtractionOutput({
   268→    surreal: runtime.surreal,
   269→    embeddingModel: runtime.embeddingModel,
   270→    embeddingDimension: runtime.config.embeddingDimension,
   271→    extractionModelId: runtime.config.extractionModelId,
   272→    extractionStoreThreshold: runtime.config.extractionStoreThreshold,
   273→    workspaceRecord,
   274→    sourceRecord: userMessageRecord as SourceRecord,
   275→    sourceKind: "message",
   276→    sourceLabel: testCase.input.slice(0, 140),
   277→    promptText: testCase.input,
   278→    output: extraction,
   279→    sourceMessageRecord: userMessageRecord,
   280→    extractionHistoryMessageIds: extractionConversationContext.conversationHistory.map(
   281→      (row) => row.id.id as string,
   282→    ),
   283→    now,
   284→  });
   285→
   286→  await appendExtractedTools(runtime.surreal, workspaceRecord, persistence.tools, now);
   287→
   288→  const [evidenceRows] = await runtime.surreal
   289→    .query<[Array<{
   290→      evidence?: string;
   291→      from_text?: string;
   292→      model?: string;
   293→      evidence_source?: RecordId<"message", string>;
   294→      resolved_from?: RecordId<"message", string>;
   295→    }>]>(
   296→      "SELECT evidence, from_text, model, evidence_source, resolved_from FROM extraction_relation WHERE `in` = $source;",
   297→      { source: userMessageRecord },
   298→    )
   299→    .collect<[Array<{
   300→      evidence?: string;
   301→      from_text?: string;
   302→      model?: string;
   303→      evidence_source?: RecordId<"message", string>;
   304→      resolved_from?: RecordId<"message", string>;
   305→    }>]>();
   306→
   307→  const personCount = await loadWorkspacePeopleCount(runtime.surreal, workspaceRecord);
   308→  const [workspaceToolRows] = await runtime.surreal
   309→    .query<[Array<{ tools?: string[] }>]>("SELECT tools FROM $workspace LIMIT 1;", {
   310→      workspace: workspaceRecord,
   311→    })
   312→    .collect<[Array<{ tools?: string[] }>]>();
   313→  const extractedTools = workspaceToolRows[0]?.tools ?? [];
   314→
   315→  const [relationRows] = await runtime.surreal
   316→    .query<[Array<{
   317→      kind: string;
   318→      in: { tb: string };
   319→      out: { tb: string };
   320→      from_text: string;
   321→      to_text: string;
   322→      confidence: number;
   323→    }>]>(
   324→      "SELECT kind, `in`, out, from_text, to_text, confidence FROM entity_relation WHERE source_message = $source AND kind != 'POSSIBLE_DUPLICATE';",
   325→      { source: userMessageRecord },
   326→    )
   327→    .collect<[Array<{
   328→      kind: string;
   329→      in: { tb: string };
   330→      out: { tb: string };
   331→      from_text: string;
   332→      to_text: string;
   333→      confidence: number;
   334→    }>]>();
   335→
   336→  const output: ExtractionEvalOutput = {
   337→    caseId: testCase.id,
   338→    input: testCase.input,
   339→    userMessageId: userMessageRecord.id as string,
   340→    contextMessageIds,
   341→    extractedEntities: persistence.entities.map((e) => ({
   342→      kind: e.kind,
   343→      text: e.text,
   344→      confidence: e.confidence,
   345→      ...(e.category ? { category: e.category } : {}),
   346→    })),
   347→    extractedTools,
   348→    personCount,
   349→    ownerPersonCount,
   350→    evidenceRows: evidenceRows.map((row) => ({
   351→      evidence: row.evidence,
   352→      fromText: row.from_text,
   353→      model: row.model,
   354→      evidenceSourceId: row.evidence_source?.id as string | undefined,
   355→      resolvedFromId: row.resolved_from?.id as string | undefined,
   356→    })),
   357→    extractedRelations: relationRows.map((row) => ({
   358→      kind: row.kind,
   359→      fromKind: row.in.tb,
   360→      fromText: row.from_text,
   361→      toKind: row.out.tb,
   362→      toText: row.to_text,
   363→      confidence: row.confidence,
   364→    })),
   365→  };
   366→
   367→  resultCache[cacheKey] = output;
   368→  saveCache(cachePath, resultCache);
   369→  return output;
   370→}
   371→
   372→function loadCache(path: string): Record<string, ExtractionEvalOutput> {
   373→  if (!existsSync(path)) {
   374→    return {};
   375→  }
   376→
   377→  return JSON.parse(readFileSync(path, "utf8")) as Record<string, ExtractionEvalOutput>;
   378→}
   379→
   380→function saveCache(path: string, cache: Record<string, ExtractionEvalOutput>): void {
   381→  mkdirSync(cacheDir, { recursive: true });
   382→  writeFileSync(path, JSON.stringify(cache, null, 2));
   383→}
   384→
   385→function assertAutoevalEnv(): void {
   386→  const hasOpenAiKey = hasEnv("OPENAI_API_KEY");
   387→  const hasBraintrustKey = hasEnv("BRAINTRUST_API_KEY");
   388→  if (!hasOpenAiKey && !hasBraintrustKey) {
   389→    throw new Error(
   390→      "Missing evaluator credentials. Set OPENAI_API_KEY (recommended for OPENAI_BASE_URL/OpenRouter) or BRAINTRUST_API_KEY.",
   391→    );
   392→  }
   393→
   394→  if (!hasEnv("OPENAI_BASE_URL")) {
   395→    throw new Error("Missing OPENAI_BASE_URL for autoevals provider routing.");
   396→  }
   397→}
   398→
   399→function requireEnv(name: string): string {
   400→  const value = process.env[name];
   401→  if (!value || value.trim().length === 0) {
   402→    throw new Error(`Missing required environment variable: ${name}`);
   403→  }
   404→  return value;
   405→}
   406→
   407→function hasEnv(name: string): boolean {
   408→  const value = process.env[name];
   409→  return value !== undefined && value.trim().length > 0;
   410→}
   411→
   412→function buildCaseCacheKey(modelId: string, testCase: GoldenCase): string {
   413→  const cacheVersion = "classification-v8";
   414→  const caseHash = createHash("sha256").update(JSON.stringify(testCase)).digest("hex").slice(0, 24);
   415→  return `${cacheVersion}:${modelId}:${testCase.id}:${caseHash}`;
   416→}
   417→
```

```
     5→export const entityPrecisionScorer = createScorer<GoldenCase, ExtractionEvalOutput, GoldenCase>({
     6→  name: "entity-precision",
     7→  description: "Percent of extracted entities that match expected entities by kind and text/text_contains expectation.",
     8→  scorer: ({ output, expected }) => {
```

```
     1→import { createScorer } from "evalite";
     2→import type { GoldenCase, ExtractionEvalOutput } from "../types";
     3→import { matchesExpectedEntity } from "./shared";
     4→
     5→export const noExtraEntitiesScorer = createScorer<GoldenCase, ExtractionEvalOutput, GoldenCase>({
     6→  name: "no-extra-entities",
     7→  description: "Strict scorer for strict_single cases: requires exact expected set with no extra entities.",
     8→  scorer: ({ output, expected }) => {
     9→    if (expected.intent !== "strict_single") {
    10→      return { score: 1 };
    11→    }
    12→
    13→    const expectedEntities = expected.expectedEntities;
    14→    const extractedEntities = output.extractedEntities;
    15→    if (expectedEntities.length !== extractedEntities.length) {
    16→      return { score: 0 };
    17→    }
    18→
    19→    const unmatchedExpected = [...expectedEntities];
    20→    for (const entity of extractedEntities) {
    21→      const matchedIndex = unmatchedExpected.findIndex((candidate) => matchesExpectedEntity(candidate, entity, 0.5));
    22→
    23→      if (matchedIndex === -1) {
    24→        return { score: 0 };
    25→      }
    26→
    27→      unmatchedExpected.splice(matchedIndex, 1);
    28→    }
    29→
    30→    return { score: unmatchedExpected.length === 0 ? 1 : 0 };
    31→  },
    32→});
    33→
```

```
     1→import type { ExpectedEntity } from "../types";
     2→
     3→function normalize(value: string): string {
     4→  return value
     5→    .toLowerCase()
     6→    .replace(/[^a-z0-9\s]/g, " ")
     7→    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
     8→    .trim();
     9→}
    10→
    11→function tokenize(value: string): string[] {
    12→  return normalize(value)
    13→    .split(" ")
    14→    .filter((token) => token.length > 0);
    15→}
    16→
    17→export function nameSimilarity(a: string, b: string): number {
    18→  const normalizedA = normalize(a);
    19→  const normalizedB = normalize(b);
    20→  const aTokens = new Set(tokenize(normalizedA));
    21→  const bTokens = new Set(tokenize(normalizedB));
    22→
    23→  if (normalizedA.length === 0 || normalizedB.length === 0 || aTokens.size === 0 || bTokens.size === 0) {
    24→    return 0;
    25→  }
    26→
    27→  const overlap = [...aTokens].filter((token) => bTokens.has(token)).length;
    28→  const minSize = Math.min(aTokens.size, bTokens.size);
    29→  const union = new Set([...aTokens, ...bTokens]).size;
    30→  const jaccard = overlap / union;
    31→  const overlapRatio = overlap / minSize;
    32→  const substringBonus = normalizedA.includes(normalizedB) || normalizedB.includes(normalizedA) ? 0.2 : 0;
    33→  const exactBonus = normalizedA === normalizedB ? 0.2 : 0;
    34→
    35→  return Math.min(1, Math.max(jaccard, overlapRatio * 0.85) + substringBonus + exactBonus);
    36→}
    37→
    38→export function isEntityNameMatch(a: string, b: string, threshold = 0.5): boolean {
    39→  return nameSimilarity(a, b) >= threshold;
    40→}
    41→
    42→export function normalizeForSubstring(value: string): string {
    43→  return normalize(value);
    44→}
    45→
    46→export function matchesExpectedEntity(
    47→  expectedEntity: ExpectedEntity,
    48→  extractedEntity: { kind: string; text: string },
    49→  threshold = 0.5,
    50→): boolean {
    51→  if (expectedEntity.kind !== extractedEntity.kind) {
    52→    return false;
    53→  }
    54→
    55→  if ("text_contains" in expectedEntity) {
    56→    const expectedSubstring = normalizeForSubstring(expectedEntity.text_contains);
    57→    if (expectedSubstring.length === 0) {
    58→      return false;
    59→    }
    60→
    61→    return normalizeForSubstring(extractedEntity.text).includes(expectedSubstring);
    62→  }
    63→
    64→  return isEntityNameMatch(expectedEntity.text, extractedEntity.text, threshold);
    65→}
    66→
```

```
     1→import type { SourceKind } from "../../shared/contracts";
     2→import { logWarn } from "../http/observability";
     3→
     4→export function resolveValidatedResolvedFromMessageId(input: {
     5→  resolvedFromMessageId?: string;
     6→  sourceKind: SourceKind;
     7→  sourceMessageId?: string;
     8→  extractionHistoryMessageIds: Set<string>;
     9→}): string | undefined {
    10→  const resolvedFromMessageId = input.resolvedFromMessageId?.trim();
    11→  if (!resolvedFromMessageId) {
    12→    return undefined;
    13→  }
    14→
    15→  if (input.sourceKind !== "message") {
    16→    throw new Error("resolvedFromMessageId is only valid for message extraction sources");
    17→  }
    18→
    19→  if (!input.sourceMessageId || input.sourceMessageId.trim().length === 0) {
    20→    throw new Error("resolvedFromMessageId requires sourceMessageId");
    21→  }
    22→
    23→  if (resolvedFromMessageId === input.sourceMessageId) {
    24→    logWarn("extraction.provenance.self_reference", "LLM returned resolvedFromMessageId matching current source message; discarding", {
    25→      resolvedFromMessageId,
    26→      sourceMessageId: input.sourceMessageId,
    27→    });
    28→    return undefined;
    29→  }
    30→
    31→  if (!input.extractionHistoryMessageIds.has(resolvedFromMessageId)) {
    32→    logWarn("extraction.provenance.unknown_message", "LLM returned resolvedFromMessageId not in conversation history; discarding", {
    33→      resolvedFromMessageId,
    34→    });
    35→    return undefined;
    36→  }
    37→
    38→  return resolvedFromMessageId;
    39→}
```

```
   207→      embeddingTargets,

   209→      unresolvedAssigneeNames: [...unresolvedAssigneeNames],

   213→      workspaceId: input.workspaceRecord.id as string,

   222→async function applyAssigneeReference(input: {
   223→  surreal: Surreal;
   224→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
   225→  entityRecord: GraphEntityRecord;
   226→  entityKind: Exclude<EntityKind, "workspace" | "person">;
   227→  assigneeName: string;
   228→  now: Date;
   229→}): Promise<{ resolved: boolean; assigneeName: string }> {
   230→  const assigneeName = input.assigneeName.trim();
   231→  if (assigneeName.length === 0) {
   232→    return { resolved: true, assigneeName };
   233→  }
   234→
   235→  const personRecord = await findWorkspacePersonByName({
   236→    surreal: input.surreal,
   237→    workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
   238→    personName: assigneeName,
   239→  });
   240→
   241→  const patch = resolvePersonAttributionPatch({
   242→    targetKind: input.entityKind,
   243→    assigneeName,
   244→    ...(personRecord ? { personRecordId: personRecord.id as string } : {}),
   245→  });
   246→
   247→  if (patch.kind === "feature") {
   248→    if (patch.field === "owner" && personRecord) {
   249→      await input.surreal.update(input.entityRecord as RecordId<"feature", string>).merge({
   250→        owner: personRecord,
   251→        updated_at: input.now,
   252→      });
   253→      return { resolved: true, assigneeName };
   254→    }
   255→
   256→    await input.surreal.update(input.entityRecord as RecordId<"feature", string>).merge({
   257→      owner_name: patch.value,
   258→      updated_at: input.now,
   259→    });
   260→    return { resolved: false, assigneeName };
   261→  }
   262→
   263→  if (patch.kind === "task") {
   264→    if (patch.field === "owner" && personRecord) {
   265→      await input.surreal.update(input.entityRecord as RecordId<"task", string>).merge({
   266→        owner: personRecord,
   267→        updated_at: input.now,
   268→      });
   269→      return { resolved: true, assigneeName };
   270→    }
   271→
   272→    await input.surreal.update(input.entityRecord as RecordId<"task", string>).merge({
   273→      owner_name: patch.value,
   274→      updated_at: input.now,
   275→    });
   276→    return { resolved: false, assigneeName };
   277→  }
   278→
   279→  if (patch.kind === "decision") {
   280→    if (patch.field === "decided_by" && personRecord) {
   281→      await input.surreal.update(input.entityRecord as RecordId<"decision", string>).merge({
   282→        decided_by: personRecord,
   283→        updated_at: input.now,
   284→      });
   285→      return { resolved: true, assigneeName };
   286→    }
   287→
   288→    await input.surreal.update(input.entityRecord as RecordId<"decision", string>).merge({
   289→      decided_by_name: patch.value,
   290→      updated_at: input.now,
   291→    });
   292→    return { resolved: false, assigneeName };
   293→  }
   294→
   295→  if (patch.field === "assigned_to" && personRecord) {
   296→    await input.surreal.update(input.entityRecord as RecordId<"question", string>).merge({
   297→      assigned_to: personRecord,
   298→      updated_at: input.now,
   299→    });
   300→    return { resolved: true, assigneeName };
   301→  }
   302→
   303→  await input.surreal.update(input.entityRecord as RecordId<"question", string>).merge({
   304→    assigned_to_name: patch.value,
   305→    updated_at: input.now,
   306→  });
   307→  return { resolved: false, assigneeName };
   308→}
   309→
   310→export async function appendExtractedTools(
   311→  surreal: Surreal,
   312→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>,
   313→  extractedTools: string[],
   314→  now: Date,
   315→): Promise<void> {
   316→  const dedupedTools = [...new Set(extractedTools.map((tool) => tool.trim()).filter((tool) => tool.length > 0))];
   317→  if (extractedTools.length > 0 && dedupedTools.length > 0) {
   318→    await appendWorkspaceTools(surreal, workspaceRecord, dedupedTools, now);
   319→  }
   320→}
```

```
     1→import type { ExtractionPromptEntity, ExtractionPromptRelationship } from "./schema";
     2→
     3→export function shouldStoreExtraction(confidence: number, threshold = 0.6): boolean {
     4→  return confidence >= threshold;
     5→}
     6→
     7→export function shouldDisplayExtraction(confidence: number, threshold = 0.85): boolean {
     8→  return confidence >= threshold;
     9→}
    10→
    11→export function hasGroundedEvidence(evidence: string, sourceText: string): boolean {
    12→  const trimmedEvidence = evidence.trim();
    13→  if (trimmedEvidence.length === 0) {
    14→    return false;
    15→  }
    16→
    17→  return sourceText.includes(trimmedEvidence);
    18→}
    19→
    20→export function postValidateEntities(input: {
    21→  entities: ExtractionPromptEntity[];
    22→  sourceText: string;
    23→  storeThreshold: number;
    24→}): ExtractionPromptEntity[] {
    25→  const byTempId = new Map<string, ExtractionPromptEntity>();
    26→
    27→  for (const entity of input.entities) {
    28→    const tempId = entity.tempId.trim();
    29→    if (tempId.length === 0) {
    30→      continue;
    31→    }
    32→
    33→    const text = entity.text.trim();
    34→    if (text.length < 3) {
    35→      continue;
    36→    }
    37→
    38→    const evidence = entity.evidence.trim();
    39→    if (evidence.length === 0) {
    40→      continue;
    41→    }
    42→
    43→    if (!shouldStoreExtraction(entity.confidence, input.storeThreshold)) {
    44→      continue;
    45→    }
    46→
    47→    if (!hasGroundedEvidence(evidence, input.sourceText)) {
    48→      continue;
    49→    }
    50→
    51→    const assigneeName = ("assignee_name" in entity ? entity.assignee_name : undefined)?.trim();
    52→    const resolvedFromMessageId = ("resolvedFromMessageId" in entity ? entity.resolvedFromMessageId : undefined)?.trim();
    53→    const normalized: ExtractionPromptEntity = {
    54→      ...entity,
    55→      tempId,
    56→      text,
    57→      evidence,
    58→      ...(assigneeName ? { assignee_name: assigneeName } : {}),
    59→      ...(resolvedFromMessageId ? { resolvedFromMessageId } : {}),
    60→    };
    61→
    62→    const existing = byTempId.get(tempId);
    63→    if (!existing || normalized.confidence > existing.confidence) {
    64→      byTempId.set(tempId, normalized);
    65→    }
    66→  }
    67→
    68→  return [...byTempId.values()];
    69→}
    70→
    71→export function postValidateRelationships(input: {
    72→  relationships: ExtractionPromptRelationship[];
    73→  storeThreshold: number;
    74→}): ExtractionPromptRelationship[] {
    75→  return input.relationships
    76→    .filter((relationship) => shouldStoreExtraction(relationship.confidence, input.storeThreshold))
    77→    .map((relationship) => {
    78→      const kind = relationship.kind.trim();
    79→      const fromTempId = relationship.fromTempId.trim();
    80→      const toTempId = relationship.toTempId.trim();
    81→      const fromText = relationship.fromText.trim();
    82→      const toText = relationship.toText.trim();
    83→
    84→      return {
    85→        ...relationship,
    86→        kind,
    87→        fromTempId,
    88→        toTempId,
    89→        fromText,
    90→        toText,
    91→      };
    92→    })
    93→    .filter(
    94→      (relationship) =>
    95→        relationship.kind.length > 0 &&
    96→        relationship.fromTempId.length > 0 &&
    97→        relationship.toTempId.length > 0 &&
    98→        relationship.fromText.length > 0 &&
    99→        relationship.toText.length > 0,
   100→    );
   101→}
   102→
```

```
     1→import { generateObject } from "ai";
     2→import {
     3→  extractionResultSchema,
     4→  type ExtractionPromptOutput,
     5→} from "./schema";
     6→import { buildExtractionSystemPrompt } from "./prompt";
     7→import type { ExtractionGraphContextRow, MessageContextRow } from "./types";
     8→import { elapsedMs, logError, logInfo } from "../http/observability";
     9→
    10→export async function extractStructuredGraph(input: {
    11→  extractionModel: any;
    12→  conversationHistory: MessageContextRow[];
    13→  currentMessage?: MessageContextRow;
    14→  graphContext: ExtractionGraphContextRow[];
    15→  sourceText: string;
    16→  onboarding: boolean;
    17→  heading?: string;
    18→  workspaceName?: string;
    19→  projectNames?: string[];
    20→}): Promise<ExtractionPromptOutput> {
    21→  const startedAt = performance.now();
    22→  logInfo("extraction.generate.started", "Structured extraction started", {
    23→    onboarding: input.onboarding,
    24→    hasHeading: input.heading !== undefined,
    25→    contextMessageCount: input.conversationHistory.length,
    26→    hasCurrentMessage: input.currentMessage !== undefined,
    27→    graphContextCount: input.graphContext.length,
    28→    sourceLength: input.sourceText.length,
    29→  });
    30→
    31→  try {
    32→    const extractionOutput = await generateObject({
    33→      model: input.extractionModel,
    34→      schema: extractionResultSchema,
    35→      temperature: 0,
    36→      system: buildExtractionSystemPrompt({ onboarding: input.onboarding }),
    37→      prompt: [
    38→        "Conversation history (reference resolution only):",
    39→        formatExtractionConversationHistory(input.conversationHistory),
    40→        "",
    41→        "Current message (extract only from this text when message metadata exists):",
    42→        formatExtractionCurrentMessage(input.currentMessage, input.sourceText),
    43→        "",
    44→        "Resolved-from lineage constraints:",
    45→        formatResolvedFromConstraints(input.currentMessage, input.conversationHistory),
    46→        "",
    47→        "Existing graph context (semantic index of prior extracted entities):",
    48→        formatExtractionGraphContext(input.graphContext),
    49→        "",
    50→        "Workspace scope:",
    51→        formatWorkspaceScope(input.workspaceName, input.projectNames),
    52→        input.heading ? `Section heading: ${input.heading}` : "",
    53→        "",
    54→        "Current source text:",
    55→        input.sourceText,
    56→      ]
    57→        .filter((line) => line.length > 0)
    58→        .join("\n"),
    59→    });
    60→
    61→    const output = extractionOutput.object as ExtractionPromptOutput;
    62→    logInfo("extraction.generate.completed", "Structured extraction completed", {
    63→      onboarding: input.onboarding,
    64→      entityCount: output.entities.length,
    65→      relationshipCount: output.relationships.length,
    66→      toolCount: output.tools.length,
    67→      durationMs: elapsedMs(startedAt),
    68→    });
    69→
    70→    return output;
    71→  } catch (error) {
    72→    logError("extraction.generate.failed", "Structured extraction failed", error, {
    73→      onboarding: input.onboarding,
    74→      durationMs: elapsedMs(startedAt),
    75→    });
    76→    throw error;
    77→  }
    78→}
    79→
    80→function formatExtractionConversationHistory(rows: MessageContextRow[]): string {
    81→  if (rows.length === 0) {
    82→    return "(no prior messages)";
    83→  }
    84→
    85→  return rows.map((row) => `[message:${row.id.id as string}] ${row.role.toUpperCase()}: ${row.text}`).join("\n");
    86→}
    87→
    88→function formatExtractionCurrentMessage(currentMessage: MessageContextRow | undefined, sourceText: string): string {
    89→  if (!currentMessage) {
    90→    return `(no message metadata; source text: ${sourceText})`;
    91→  }
    92→
    93→  return `[message:${currentMessage.id.id as string}] ${currentMessage.role.toUpperCase()}: ${currentMessage.text}`;
    94→}
    95→
    96→function formatExtractionGraphContext(rows: ExtractionGraphContextRow[]): string {
    97→  if (rows.length === 0) {
    98→    return "(no prior extracted entities)";
    99→  }
   100→
   101→  return rows
   102→    .map((row) => {
   103→      const table = row.id.tb;
   104→      return `[entity:${table}:${row.id.id as string}] ${row.kind}: ${row.text} (confidence ${row.confidence.toFixed(2)}, source message ${row.sourceMessage.id as string})`;
   105→    })
   106→    .join("\n");
   107→}
   108→
   109→function formatWorkspaceScope(workspaceName?: string, projectNames?: string[]): string {
   110→  if (!workspaceName) {
   111→    return "(no workspace context)";
   112→  }
   113→
   114→  const lines = [`Workspace: "${workspaceName}"`];
   115→  if (projectNames && projectNames.length > 0) {
   116→    lines.push(`Existing projects: ${projectNames.map((name) => `"${name}"`).join(", ")}`);
   117→  } else {
   118→    lines.push("Existing projects: none");
   119→  }
   120→
   121→  return lines.join("\n");
   122→}
   123→
   124→function formatResolvedFromConstraints(
   125→  currentMessage: MessageContextRow | undefined,
   126→  conversationHistory: MessageContextRow[],
   127→): string {
   128→  if (!currentMessage) {
   129→    return "Current message id unavailable; omit resolvedFromMessageId.";
   130→  }
   131→
   132→  const currentMessageId = currentMessage.id.id as string;
   133→  if (conversationHistory.length === 0) {
   134→    return [
   135→      `Current message id (forbidden for resolvedFromMessageId): ${currentMessageId}`,
   136→      "Allowed resolvedFromMessageId values (history only): none",
   137→      "Omit resolvedFromMessageId.",
   138→    ].join("\n");
   139→  }
   140→
   141→  const historyMessageIds = conversationHistory.map((row) => row.id.id as string);
   142→  return [
   143→    `Current message id (forbidden for resolvedFromMessageId): ${currentMessageId}`,
   144→    `Allowed resolvedFromMessageId values (history only): ${historyMessageIds.join(", ")}`,
   145→    "Use one of the allowed ids exactly, or omit resolvedFromMessageId.",
   146→  ].join("\n");
   147→}
   148→
```

```
    11→export function createDeterministicIdGenerator(seed: string): () => string {
    12→  let counter = 0;
    13→  return () => {
    14→    const hash = createHash("sha256").update(`${seed}:${counter++}`).digest("hex");
    15→    return `${hash.slice(0, 8)}-${hash.slice(8, 12)}-${hash.slice(12, 16)}-${hash.slice(16, 20)}-${hash.slice(20, 32)}`;
    16→  };
    17→}

    19→export type EvalRuntime = {

    21→  extractionModel: any;
    22→  embeddingModel: any;
    23→  assistantModel: any;
    24→  config: ServerConfig;
    25→  namespace: string;
    26→  database: string;

    31→const surrealPassword = process.env.SURREAL_PASSWORD ?? "root";
    32→const schemaPath = join(process.cwd(), "schema", "surreal-schema.surql");
    33→
    34→export async function setupEvalRuntime(suiteName: string): Promise<EvalRuntime> {
    35→  const namespace = `eval_${Date.now()}_${Math.floor(Math.random() * 100000)}`;
    36→  const database = `${suiteName}_${Math.floor(Math.random() * 100000)}`;
    37→
    38→  const surreal = new Surreal();
    39→  await surreal.connect(surrealUrl);
    40→  await surreal.signin({ username: surrealUsername, password: surrealPassword });
    41→
    42→  await surreal.query(`DEFINE NAMESPACE ${namespace};`).catch((error) => {
    43→    if (!isAlreadyExistsError(error)) throw error;
    44→  });
    45→  await surreal.use({ namespace, database });
    46→  await surreal.query(`REMOVE DATABASE ${database};`).catch(() => undefined);
    47→  await surreal.query(`DEFINE DATABASE ${database};`).catch((error) => {
    48→    if (!isAlreadyExistsError(error)) throw error;
    49→  });
    50→  await surreal.use({ namespace, database });
    51→
    52→  const schemaSql = readFileSync(schemaPath, "utf8");
    53→  await surreal.query(schemaSql).catch((error) => {
    54→    if (!isAlreadyExistsError(error)) throw error;
    55→  });
    56→
    57→  const openRouterApiKey = requireEnv("OPENROUTER_API_KEY");
    58→  const extractionModelId = process.env.EXTRACTION_MODEL ?? "anthropic/claude-3.5-haiku";
    59→  const assistantModelId = process.env.ASSISTANT_MODEL ?? "anthropic/claude-3.5-haiku";
    60→  const embeddingModelId = requireEnv("OPENROUTER_EMBEDDING_MODEL");
    61→  const embeddingDimension = Number(requireEnv("EMBEDDING_DIMENSION"));
    62→  const extractionStoreThreshold = Number(process.env.EXTRACTION_STORE_THRESHOLD ?? "0.3");
    63→  const extractionDisplayThreshold = Number(process.env.EXTRACTION_DISPLAY_THRESHOLD ?? "0.5");
    64→
    65→  const openrouter = createOpenRouter({ apiKey=[REDACTED] });
    66→  const extractionModel = openrouter(extractionModelId, { plugins: [{ id: "response-healing" }] });
    67→  const assistantModel = openrouter(assistantModelId, { plugins: [{ id: "response-healing" }] });
    68→  const embeddingModel = openrouter.textEmbeddingModel(embeddingModelId);
    69→
    70→  const config: ServerConfig = {
    71→    openRouterApiKey,
    72→    assistantModelId,
    73→    extractionModelId,
    74→    embeddingModelId,
    75→    embeddingDimension,
    76→    extractionStoreThreshold,

    82→    surrealDatabase: database,

    84→  };

    86→  return {

    89→    embeddingModel,

    93→    database,

    95→}

   100→  await runtime.surreal.close().catch(() => undefined);

   103→export async function seedWorkspace(surreal: Surreal, workspaceName?: string, nextId?: () => string): Promise<{
   104→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
   105→  workspaceName: string;
   106→  projectRecord: RecordId<"project", string>;
   107→  conversationRecord: RecordId<"conversation", string>;
   108→  ownerPersonCount: number;
   109→}> {
   110→  const id = nextId ?? randomUUID;
   111→  const now = new Date();
   112→  const resolvedWorkspaceName = workspaceName ?? `Eval ${Date.now()}`;
   113→  const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", id());
   114→  const projectRecord = new RecordId("project", id());
   115→  const conversationRecord = new RecordId("conversation", id());
   116→  const ownerRecord = new RecordId("person", id());
   117→
   118→  await surreal.create(workspaceRecord).content({
   119→    name: resolvedWorkspaceName,
   120→    status: "active",
   121→    onboarding_complete: false,
   122→    onboarding_turn_count: 0,
   123→    onboarding_summary_pending: false,
   124→    onboarding_started_at: now,
   125→    created_at: now,
   126→    updated_at: now,
   127→  });
   128→
   129→  await surreal.create(projectRecord).content({
   130→    name: "Eval Project",
   131→    status: "active",
   132→    created_at: now,
   133→    updated_at: now,
   134→  });
   135→
   136→  // NOTE: Do NOT link default eval project to workspace via has_project.
   137→  // Only workspace_seed projects should appear in workspace scope so the
   138→  // extraction LLM sees the correct project list for project-vs-feature classification.
   139→
   140→  await surreal.create(ownerRecord).content({
   141→    name: "Marcus",
   142→    created_at: now,
   143→    updated_at: now,
   144→  });
   145→
   146→  await surreal.relate(ownerRecord, new RecordId("member_of", id()), workspaceRecord, {
   147→    role: "owner",
   148→    added_at: now,
   149→  }).output("after");
   150→
   151→  await surreal.create(conversationRecord).content({
   152→    createdAt: now,
   153→    updatedAt: now,
   154→    workspace: workspaceRecord,
   155→    source: "onboarding",
   156→  });
   157→
   158→  return { workspaceRecord, workspaceName: resolvedWorkspaceName, projectRecord, conversationRecord, ownerPersonCount: 1 };
   159→}
   160→
   161→export async function seedConversationContext(
   162→  surreal: Surreal,
   163→  conversationRecord: RecordId<"conversation", string>,
   164→  context: Array<{ role: "user" | "assistant"; text: string }>,
   165→  nextId?: () => string,
   166→): Promise<string[]> {
   167→  const id = nextId ?? randomUUID;
   168→  const now = Date.now();
   169→  const messageIds: string[] = [];
   170→  for (const [index, message] of context.entries()) {
   171→    const seededMessageRecord = new RecordId("message", id());
   172→    await surreal.create(seededMessageRecord).content({
   173→      conversation: conversationRecord,
   174→      role: message.role,
   175→      text: message.text,
   176→      createdAt: new Date(now + index),
   177→    });
   178→    messageIds.push(seededMessageRecord.id as string);
   179→  }
   180→  return messageIds;
   181→}
   182→
   183→export async function seedUserMessage(
   184→  surreal: Surreal,
   185→  conversationRecord: RecordId<"conversation", string>,
   186→  text: string,
   187→  nextId?: () => string,
   188→): Promise<RecordId<"message", string>> {
   189→  const messageRecord = new RecordId("message", (nextId ?? randomUUID)());
   190→  await surreal.create(messageRecord).content({
   191→    conversation: conversationRecord,

   199→export async function loadWorkspacePeopleCount(
   200→  surreal: Surreal,
   201→  workspace: RecordId<"workspace", string>,
   202→): Promise<number> {
   203→  const [people] = await surreal
   204→    .query<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"person", string> }>]>(
   205→      "SELECT id FROM person WHERE id IN (SELECT VALUE `in` FROM member_of WHERE out = $workspace);",
   206→      { workspace },
   207→    )
   208→    .collect<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"person", string> }>]>();
   209→  return people.length;
   210→}
   211→
   212→
   213→export async function seedGraphEntities(
   214→  surreal: Surreal,
   215→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>,
   216→  projectRecord: RecordId<"project", string>,
   217→  conversationRecord: RecordId<"conversation", string>,
   218→  seeds: WorkspaceSeedItem[],
   219→  nextId?: () => string,
   220→): Promise<void> {
   221→  const id = nextId ?? randomUUID;
   222→  const now = new Date();
   223→  const seedMessageRecord = new RecordId("message", id());
   224→  await surreal.create(seedMessageRecord).content({
   225→    conversation: conversationRecord,
   226→    role: "assistant",
   227→    text: "Workspace seed context.",
   228→    createdAt: new Date(now.getTime() - 60_000),
   229→  });
   230→
   231→  for (const seed of seeds) {
   232→    const entityRecord = new RecordId(seed.kind, id());
   233→    await surreal.create(entityRecord).content(buildSeedEntityContent(seed.kind, seed.text, now));
   234→
   235→    await surreal.relate(seedMessageRecord, new RecordId("extraction_relation", id()), entityRecord, {
   236→      confidence: 0.95,
   237→      extracted_at: now,
   238→      created_at: now,
   239→      model: "seed",
   240→      from_text: seed.text,
   241→      evidence: seed.text,
   242→      evidence_source: seedMessageRecord,
   243→    }).output("after");
   244→
   245→    if (seed.kind === "feature") {
   246→      await surreal.relate(projectRecord, new RecordId("has_feature", id()), entityRecord as RecordId<"feature", string>, {
   247→        added_at: now,
   248→      }).output("after");
   249→    }
   250→
   251→    if (seed.kind === "task" || seed.kind === "decision" || seed.kind === "question") {
   252→      await surreal.relate(entityRecord, new RecordId("belongs_to", id()), projectRecord, {
   253→        added_at: now,
   254→      }).output("after");
   255→    }
   256→
   257→    if (seed.kind === "project") {
   258→      await surreal.relate(workspaceRecord, new RecordId("has_project", id()), entityRecord as RecordId<"project", string>, {
   259→        added_at: now,
   260→      }).output("after");
   261→    }
   262→  }
   263→}
   264→
   265→function buildSeedEntityContent(kind: string, text: string, now: Date): Record<string, unknown> {
   266→  const base = { created_at: now, updated_at: now };
   267→  if (kind === "project") return { ...base, name: text, status: "active" };
   268→  if (kind === "feature") return { ...base, name: text, status: "active" };
   269→  if (kind === "task") return { ...base, title: text, status: "open" };
   270→  if (kind === "decision") return { ...base, summary: text, status: "extracted" };
   271→  return { ...base, text, status: "open" };
   272→}
   273→
   274→export function createEventCollector(): SseRegistry & { getEvents(messageId: string): StreamEvent[] } {
   275→  const events = new Map<string, StreamEvent[]>();
   276→  return {
   277→    registerMessage(messageId: string) {
   278→      events.set(messageId, []);
   279→    },
   280→    handleStreamRequest(_messageId: string): Response {
   281→      throw new Error("handleStreamRequest not available in eval mode");
   282→    },
   283→    emitEvent(messageId: string, event: StreamEvent) {
   284→      events.get(messageId)?.push(event);
   285→    },
   286→    getEvents(messageId: string): StreamEvent[] {
   287→      return events.get(messageId) ?? [];
   288→    },
   289→  };
   290→}
   291→
   292→function isAlreadyExistsError(error: unknown): boolean {
   293→  return error instanceof Error && error.message.includes("already exists");
   294→}
   295→
   296→function requireEnv(name: string): string {
   297→  const value = process.env[name];
   298→  if (!value || value.trim().length === 0) {
   299→    throw new Error(`Missing required environment variable: ${name}`);
   300→  }
   301→  return value;
   302→}
```

> AGENT

Here's a summary of all the changes made:

### Prompt changes (`app/src/server/extraction/prompt.ts`)

**New rules added:**
1. **Feature verb expansion** (line 34): Added `we need, requires` to feature classification triggers — fixes `feature-capability-under-project` (85%) where "We need CSV export" wasn't recognized
2. **Explicit kind label respect** (line 38): When user says "the main feature is X", respect that label — fixes `feature-basic` (72%) where it was misclassified as project
3. **Task subject suppression** (line 39): Don't extract a task's subject as a separate entity — fixes `category-task-research` (86%) where "pricing models" was over-extracted as a project
4. **Conjunction extraction** (lines 79-81): New section requiring separate entities for items joined by "and"/"commas" — fixes `project-no-keyword-launch` (92%), `feature-within-project` (92%), `project-with-features-inline` (93%)
5. **Relationship to existing graph entities** (line 113): Emit RELATES_TO when extracted entity references existing entity — fixes `decision-with-feature-link` (88%)

**New examples added** (lines 100-105):
- "The main feature is X" → feature (explicit label)
- "research pricing models" → task only (no separate project)
- "payment processing and onboarding flows" → two separate projects
- "CSV export and PDF generation under existing project" → two features
- "notification system needs alerts and digests" → project + 2 features
- Updated event sourcing example to show RELATES_TO pattern

**Infrastructure:**
- Cache version bumped to `classification-v9` to force re-evaluation

Please run `bun run eval` to see the updated scores.

```
+export function createDeterministicIdGenerator(seed: string): () => string {
+  let counter = 0;
+  return () => {
+    const hash = createHash("sha256").update(`${seed}:${counter++}`).digest("hex");
+    return `${hash.slice(0, 8)}-${hash.slice(8, 12)}-${hash.slice(12, 16)}-${hash.slice(16, 20)}-${hash.slice(20, 32)}`;
+  };
+}
```

Score went from 95% to 95% overall but the mix changed. Let me analyze:

**Fixes that worked:**
- feature-basic: 72% → 95% (kind label respect)
- feature-capability-under-project: 85% → 95% ("we need" trigger)
- category-task-research: 86% → 95% (task subject suppression)
- assistant-context-not-source: 89% → 97%

**New regression:**
- category-decision-design: 95% → 63% (extracts 2 features instead of 1 decision - "We'll go with" ignored, "onboarding flow" triggers feature)

**Unchanged:**
- decision-with-feature-link: 88% (relation dropped during persistence - infrastructure issue)
- project-no-keyword-launch: 92% (still 1/2 - drops second item)
- feature-within-project: 92% (still 1/2 - drops SSO)
- project-with-features-inline: 93% (still 2/3 - drops "digests")

The regression is because selection language ("go with X instead of Y") is being treated as two separate features. Let me fix that and try to strengthen the conjunction rule.

The model now extracts 2 features ("single-page onboarding flow" + "multi-step onboarding flow") instead of 1 decision. The "flow" artifact hint in the feature rule is overriding the "We'll go with" decision language.

I need to:
1. Fix the regression by strengthening decision priority over feature when selection language is present
2. Add an explicit example for "X instead of Y" selection patterns

```
   412→function buildCaseCacheKey(modelId: string, testCase: GoldenCase): string {
   413→  const cacheVersion = "classification-v9";
   414→  const caseHash = createHash("sha256").update(JSON.stringify(testCase)).digest("hex").slice(0, 24);
   415→  return `${cacheVersion}:${modelId}:${testCase.id}:${caseHash}`;
   416→}
```

> DEVELOPER

The prompt needs a clearer rule for this case:
When a statement expresses a CHOICE between alternatives ("X instead of Y", 
"we'll go with X", "decided on X over Y"), extract as a single Decision — 
even if the chosen option sounds like a feature. 

"Single-page onboarding flow instead of multi-step" = 1 Decision
NOT 1 Decision + 1 Feature called "single-page onboarding flow"

The test: did the user decide something, or describe something to build?
- "We'll go with single-page onboarding" → Decision (chose between options)
- "We need to build a single-page onboarding flow" → Feature (describing work)
Add this as an eval case too — the test should assert exactly 1 entity of kind decision.

```
     1→import { createScorer } from "evalite";
     2→import type { GoldenCase, ExtractionEvalOutput } from "../types";
     3→
     4→export const forbiddenKindsScorer = createScorer<GoldenCase, ExtractionEvalOutput, GoldenCase>({
     5→  name: "forbidden-kinds",
     6→  description: "Score 1 when extracted entities do not include forbidden kinds for the case.",
     7→  scorer: ({ output, expected }) => {
     8→    const forbiddenKinds = expected.forbiddenExtractedKinds ?? [];
     9→    if (forbiddenKinds.length === 0) {
    10→      return { score: 1 };
    11→    }
    12→
    13→    const foundForbiddenKinds = output.extractedEntities
    14→      .map((entity) => entity.kind)
    15→      .filter((kind) => forbiddenKinds.includes(kind as (typeof forbiddenKinds)[number]));
    16→
    17→    return {
    18→      score: foundForbiddenKinds.length === 0 ? 1 : 0,
    19→      metadata: foundForbiddenKinds.length === 0 ? undefined : { foundForbiddenKinds },
    20→    };
    21→  },
    22→});
    23→
```
